spec:E30-D06 — Engineering hand-off pack for the order ticket (SCR-060, SCR-061, SCR-076, SCR-077, SCR-113 trading rows, SCR-114, SCR-158) › 6. spec:E30-D06 — Engineering hand-off pack for the order ticket (SCR-060, SCR-061, SCR-076, SCR-077, SCR-113 trading rows, SCR-114, SCR-158) › 6. spec:E30-D06 — Engineering hand-off pack for the order ticket (SCR-060, SCR-061, SCR-076, SCR-077, SCR-113 trading rows, SCR-114, SCR-158) › 6. The copy deck (`E30-D06-copy.json`; AC2; BQ-11..BQ-15) › 6.1 The key scheme and the `errors.ts` map (BQ-12)#1	s6	- **Parameters** (RULED row 1): every placeholder is a typed, identifier-safe parameter (`{environment}` for D05's `{LIVE/DEMO}`, `{retry_at}` for `{hh:mm:ss}`, `{arm_key}`, `{visible_label}` and so on; the rendering noted, the parent's spelling kept as `parent`); no BUY, SELL, "rises to" or "falls to" outside a parameter (row 12): `{side}` and `{direction}` compose per D02:310 and D02:243, recorded on the submit labels as `compose`.
spec:E30-D06 — Engineering hand-off pack for the order ticket (SCR-060, SCR-061, SCR-076, SCR-077, SCR-113 trading rows, SCR-114, SCR-158) › 6.
